# GAP-2 — first-release coverage closure

Date: 2026-10-05 (UTC). Result: **122-ID inventory reconciled; four defects retained as failing GAP tests. No production changes.**

Frozen staging: `claude/verify-terminal-20261001@889c9ebd523cbfedb39d9bf06e22ba7b9988ea46`. Delivery branch: `codex/gap-2`, created directly from that fetched head; no pending implementation was merged. GAP-1 report/tests were read from `codex/gap-1@9aa75673f6e600ca02acf2618075f0ac8c3b3519`. Upstream authority remains B3 `52bc7926097cd622d08d6294e0d8b08a48c7516e`.

## GAP-2 result and counting rules

This is a copy of GAP-1 with the affected acceptance/BUILD/crosswalk table rows updated. GAP-1's other findings, branch snapshot and inventories remain historical context; its four GAP test files were inspected, not imported wholesale. This task covers its **122 missing exact acceptance locators** for owning units already merged at the frozen staging head. A locator does not certify all native/manual behavior. No pending feature was merged, no existing assertion changed, and no production file changed.

| Missing-ID inventory | Tagged existing tests | New test methods / cases | Defects | Out of scope |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Voice (34) | 8 | 2 / 4 | 1 | 26 |
| Customization (70) | 35 | 4 / 5 | 2 | 35 |
| On-demand skills (18) | 11 | 1 / 1 | 1 | 7 |
| **Total (122)** | **54** | **7 / 10** | **4** | **68** |

Counts overlap: new tests strengthen IDs that also received existing-test tags, and defects are within the 54 tagged IDs. Out of scope comprises **67 IDs on unmerged/unbuilt units plus one manual gate (DL-20)**. The existing-test edits add **175 Spec attributes across 101 files**, for 61 distinct IDs: the 54 missing IDs plus seven supplemental partial IDs (CZ-T11/T14/T17/T18/T19/T27 and DL-16). Class traits are limited to focused suites; mixed diagnostic suites were not broadly tagged. The new tests add seven further Spec attributes.

## Scope and interpretation

The tables enumerate numbered V/CZ clauses, every V-T/CZ-T acceptance ID, numbered skills subsections and WU/DL lists, all strike K IDs and numbered sections, and the requested BUILD units. Subsection rows include their subordinate bullets/formulas; acceptance rows are a separate inventory, so do not sum both inventories as atomic requirements. Sources/history/open questions have no executable acceptance ID and are identified as documentation. Four independent source reviewers covered voice, customization, skills, and strike/other BUILD units; the lead reconciled their findings and wrote the tests. This is test assertion coverage, not Windows acceptance or a parity certification.

`tests/Marrow.Testing/Traceability.cs` includes method **and declaring-type** traits. A Spec trait locates a test but does not prove its assertion covers the whole requirement. Comments, test names, Feature/Review/Decision/Rule traits alone are listed as **untagged candidates**, never silently credited. A partial row has a located test that checks only part of the obligation. `missing` commonly means **missing Spec traceability**, not missing implementation. Covered-on-unmerged requires both adequate assertions and a non-ancestor branch; copies on an unmerged branch do not displace coverage already on staging.

`STATE.md` at this head names no pending `codex/*` or `cloud/*` refs. The audit therefore fetched **all** published refs in both namespaces, and inspected every `f-*` feature ref plus `codex/verify-cz-7a` and `cloud/review-ue-6` (49 ownership/review snapshots, 22 ancestors and 27 unmerged). The frozen branch table below is the actual evidence. The expected newer STATE/BUILD inventory is a documentation gap: **WL units and their X-Tn IDs do not exist in the frozen BUILD-PLAN**, including the inspected WL-5 copy. No wallpaper acceptance requirements or invented test IDs are fabricated. Pending UE-6 aliases are recorded separately and do not replace UE-1…5.

Later owner rulings in BUILD take precedence over provisional recommendations: picked microphone input, CUDA optional download, consent, 30-second countdown, Return-equivalent send, separate Skills/Plugins pages and download terms. Superseded shipped/patched-bundle rules in skills-PLAN are listed for completeness but excluded from first-release failures; GAP tests do not demand that vendor files ship again.

## GAP-1 findings retained as context

- There are **122 missing exact acceptance locators** across voice (34), customization (70), and on-demand skills (18), even after pending branches are considered. Existing substantive tests frequently omit Spec metadata.
- Voice V-T51/V-T62 permit allocation budgets of 1,536 B/frame and 9/5 KiB instead of 0 B. New real renderer regressions expose the discrepancy. V-T104 tests a backing flag, not composer integration; V-T80 tests intent rather than actual Return-equivalent send. V-T89 uses the unit's own speaker script because the locked design script is unavailable. V-T46 leaves several seam-default operations uninvoked. V-T90 misses the functional cloud key action, which remains disabled.
- Customization's six located but incomplete IDs check Core helpers instead of sliders, folds, Reset, focus/inertness or actual AppSettings coalescing. Untagged App tests provide valuable additional evidence; these are primarily traceability gaps. Pending CZ-T91 also allows two writes where one coalesced write is required.
- Strike still uses the earlier A2 path: Mix/starts/plume/ink modules are absent and bubble contact rejects opacity below .12 instead of .03. Locked strike-port vector inputs and TESTS.md are absent; existing `tests/fixtures/strike` is **the earlier A2 oracle**, not proof of Mix/Flash-streak requirements.
- UE-3 adapters and trial-H command fixtures are absent; GR-2's saturation is undrawn; deferred Welcome/orb integration still needs an actual composer assertion. DL-19 has adequate fetch-sharing/restart assertions **only on unmerged cloud/f-sk-16**.

## Section 19 performance requirements

| Requirement | Unit | Coverage status | Remaining proof |
|---|---|---|---|
| Capture + processing <2% one core | VT-5/VT-6 | missing | Native quiet-PC trace; no V Spec test asserts CPU percentage. |
| UI frame <1.5ms | VT-10/VT-12 | missing | Measure actual composer clock while session runs. |
| Orb draw <0.5ms GPU, 0 B/frame | VT-8 | covered but the test does not actually check the requirement | V-T51 permits allocations; native GPU tests have no Spec. Direct GC-byte GAP test mechanical; native timing manual. |
| Glow draw <2ms GPU, 0 B/frame | VT-9 | covered but the test does not actually check the requirement | V-T62 permits 5/9 KiB; native timings no Spec. Direct allocation GAP test mechanical. |
| Frame gaps under Vulkan; CPU fallback if gate fails | VT-6/VT-12 | missing | Run actual inference + native composer frame-gap gate, verify fallback. |
| First word 0.3–0.7s | VT-6/VT-12 | missing | Real approved fixture/model on owner PC; scripted arrival cannot prove latency. |
| Final decode <0.5s + schedule | VT-6/VT-12 | missing | Native voice-evidence measurement with fixture/model, schedule math already covered V-T25. |

Section 20 is the superseded phase sequencing, now split into BUILD-PLAN VT-0..VT-17; it creates no extra behavioral acceptance IDs. Section 22 merge records are documentation obligations: natives/clip/pins/dependencies/decisions are partly in staging, but real orb/glow/capture/Whisper/VAD native evidence and complete parity/UI/settings mapping are manual review gaps. Section 23 lists provisional questions; use the newer owner decisions and BUILD-PLAN rulings instead of testing superseded recommendations.

## Mechanical GAP proposals and manual descriptions

1. Every missing acceptance id: coverage test must find a non-GAP Fact/Theory bearing the exact Spec id in the frozen staging or owning pending branch. This catches the locating gap without pretending an implemented behavior is absent. Do not add Spec traits to production tests in this report-only packet.
2. V-T51 / V-11.3 / V-19: prewarm OrbRenderer and target surface; call actual renderer.Draw inside GC.GetAllocatedBytesForCurrentThread measurement; assert aggregate allocation ==0, with no test fixtures/bitmap output inside measured loop. Existing test scaffold shows this should fail because Skia uniforms/wrappers allocate.
3. V-T62 / V-13.3 / V-19: prewarm VoiceGlowPainter with an existing SKSurface/frame/look; assert actual 20-frame painter.Draw aggregate allocation ==0 for analytic/stroked modes. Existing test scaffold measures 3.7–7.4KiB/frame.
4. V-T104 / V-14.3: main/compact/Hydra composer test must find one voice slot when startup enables flag, and zero when explicitly disabled. This can be a mechanical source/traceability gap on staging until composer implementations are integrated; do not invent missing symbols to break compilation.
5. V-T90 / V-16.3.1: in existing headless VoiceTypingPageRig, select cloud, settle, assert KeyButton.IsEnabled and accessible password box (placeholder Paste your OpenAI key). Current test explicitly asserts disabled => real failing assertion can expose the gap.
6. V-T80 / V-8.8: actual running-turn and working-head composer send paths need Spec-tagged tests; the existing pending ComposerVoiceSendTests are strong supplemental evidence but cannot count by Spec until mapped. Manual/coverage description on current staging without integrated view.
7. V-T89 / V-14.8: assert an exact design SCRIPT/SYN/STREAM fixture plus committed generator exists and replay equal seeded waveform/word schedule; current own four-word script proves only mechanism. Pending VT-11 dictation-stream fixture may supply words but no Spec-tagged whole source fixture proves waveform/seeds or preview draft isolation.
8. V-4.7 / V-17.1: retain the rented capture/session float array across finish/cancel and assert all used sample bytes are zero before return to pool. BufferedAudio==TimeSpan.Zero alone is insufficient. Probe File/Trace/console writes through authorized seams to check privacy; native OS writes remain manual.
9. V-3.2: pinned native byte sum is 61,957,120 (Vulkan DLL alone 58,013,696), exceeding the stated 50 MB estimate. It is reported as a budget discrepancy, not converted into a new mandatory failing expectation. Installed native/VC++ validation requires Windows.
10. V-12.12 / V-T131..133: require actual pill snapshots at all set/state/DPI/size/orb-motion combinations and the stated glyph/annulus/speck image assertions. No pending VT-12 branch was found; render/manual gap, not a permissive placeholder test.

## Mechanical gap proposals and native follow-up

For every strict missing acceptance id, a GAP_ coverage test can require an existing non-GAP Fact/Theory carrying that Spec trait. These tests accurately fail for traceability omissions without claiming production misbehavior. For the six partial ids, require Spec-tagged tests that exercise the omitted boundary, or add tags to the already substantive candidate tests listed above after checking the assertions; do not label the existing helper-only coverage complete.

- CZ-T11: show Corner with stored radius 30; verify knob at max 26 and store not rewritten until an actual user edit.
- CZ-T14: render Pulse/Mono/Hold-still rows and assert opacity .5, no focus target, and pointer/key edit cannot change settings.
- CZ-T17: activate Reset from non-default family/shape/palette, and verify default state then idle .42/unfocusable control.
- CZ-T18: change scheme through the actual page control and inspect Your colours fold in Custom and in each other scheme.
- CZ-T19: create actual Uiverse Speed control and test bounds, .5 step, inversion and formatted value.
- CZ-T27: drive actual AppSettings-backed page drag for 120 frames, assert each in-memory value and <=9 writes plus final flush.
- CZ-T60..68: none of the reviewed branches supplies the sticky implementation tests. Direct mechanical tests should exercise layout transforms, hit testing and actual keyboard focus; a formula test detached from the page is insufficient.
- CZ-T69, CZ-16 native performance: on quiet owner PC, warm GPU, scroll Customization continuously at 60 Hz; record frame-gap gate, UI-thread scroll work <1 ms, first-frame open <150 ms, visibility/minimize frame stopping and allocation probes.

**Additional untagged assertion weakness:** `origin/codex/f-cz-7a:tests/Marrow.App.Tests/Port/Settings/CustomizationVoiceRowsTests.cs`, `CZ_T91_Every_language_pick_including_current_clears_override_and_keeps_other_settings`, accepts `Assert.InRange(writeDelta, 1, 2)` where CZ-T91 requires one coalesced write. This is still classified strict missing; even adding a Spec trait would leave the two-write allowance unproven.

## Mechanical GAP suggestions and manual items

1. For DL-0…14, DL-17, DL-18: source/reflective coverage test must find a Fact/Theory effective Spec trait (method + declaring class) on staging or owning pending branch. Existing candidates are mostly real useful tests, but the locating contract is absent. Alias SK-T11/SK-T12 must not silently substitute for DL-11/DL-12 all-provider coverage.
2. For DL-15 actual footer: inspect the mounted LibraryPage footer text/links in headless Skills and Plugins pages under SK-15, rather than only a constants test. Existing LibraryPageTests candidate has Decision only. A metadata GAP demanding Spec DL-15 on the actual control assertion can fail without inventing a production defect.
3. For DL-16 Reset: the behavior already has a strong direct host test (see Reset_through_the_host...; it invokes ResetAsync, reopens host, asserts enabled text and HasAccepted(hash)). Add a failing locating coverage check for Spec DL-16 on this predicate; do not claim Reset behavior is unimplemented. Native ACL/held-handle/10 s tests are separate Windows proof. WU-18(b) full --veloapp-uninstall dispatch remains manual/untested.
4. WU-7 resumed thread path: located Spec SK-T7 tests check moved-note formatting/ResumeParameters and helper repeated Register calls. They do not run end+restart+thread/resume and verify the old transcript script exists when a resumed turn starts. Mechanical fake app-server test would create generation, run/end session, reopen coordinator, resume, inspect extraRoots and fixture rollout script path; branch-only until wiring merged.
5. DL-20/WU-11: no fixture test substitutes for live vendor downloads/signature pins, real embeddable Python/pip site target, LibreOffice non-elevated MSI, Chromium layout, CommonJS/ESM NODE_PATH, CLI elevated sandbox/ACLs, real provider skill budgets and Antigravity/Cursor plugin evidence. Record the absent artifacts explicitly. WU-4 150 ms per-launch budget is also manual Windows machine proof.
6. WU-15/design: owner canvas approval/locked copy is an artifact check, not a fake passing assertion. Conditional obsolete WU-19/20 and withdrawn WU-1/2/3 are not release failures.

Mechanical GAP test proposals: require reflected types `StrikePlan`, `StrikeStarts`, `StrikeInk`, `StrikePlume`; require `Strike.Ss` and 1025-entry `Strike.Ease` lookup; independently calculate Mix stormAt and compare live scene timing; inject/locate a low-alpha (0.03 to 0.12) intersecting bubble and assert it bursts. Each shared boundary test addresses every item requiring that absent subsystem; its failure proves the missing boundary, **not all downstream formulas**. Each numerical row above remains missing until vector or independent formula assertions exist.

Native/manual: §2.3 and §12 require render-thread counters, zero allocation and GPU timing at both scales, quiet 240 Hz pacing (99%, max gap 21 ms, no GC 60 s, zero main frames); §6.4 U-06 requires the owner eye check of refracted bubbles; recover hash-pinned locked engine/page and vectors for Phase A–D. §13 questions are documented, with U-02…U-05/U-07 decisions to be resolved against `engineering_decisions.md`, not failing product assertions against proposals.

## GAP-2 verification and delivery

Linux Debian 13 x64, .NET SDK **10.0.401**, Avalonia **12.1.2**. The final solution build succeeds with **0 warnings / 0 errors** using `dotnet build Marrow.sln --no-restore -p:EnableWindowsTargeting=true`. The older 10.0.100 compiler hit a nullable-analysis error in existing production code; installing the current .NET 10 SDK resolved it without a source edit.

Every primary test command includes `&FullyQualifiedName!~Native` and a fresh temporary `MARROW_PORT_DATA_DIR`. Filters use fully qualified namespaces/classes with a trailing `.` to avoid accidentally selecting unrelated `TextGenerationTests` or Windows-only similarly named classes. All touched non-Native classes plus each assembly's TraceabilityTests are selected. The Native GPU warm-up test was tagged but excluded on Linux. Existing skip attributes remain intact.

| Primary slice | Passed | Failed | Skipped | Meaning |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Core | 396 | 1 | 0 | DL-1 strict embedded catalogue regression |
| Services | 447 | 1 | 3 | V-T43 `.partial` regression; three pre-existing Windows/process-related skips |
| App | 111 | 30 | 0 | CZ-T31 and CZ-T40 assertion defects; 26 glyphTypeface failures and two missing `icu.dll` blocks |
| UI | 3 | 0 | 0 | Traceability only; no UI sources changed; native GPU test excluded in App |
| **Total** | **957** | **32** | **3** | **992 selected cases** |
| Traceability (included above) | **12** | **0** | **0** | All four assemblies pass |
| Gate B | | | | **8 PASS, exit 0** |

The 28 additional App failures do not establish new Windows product defects: 26 throw `Could not create glyphTypeface` for Inter/Cascadia/default faces, often while `HeadlessUnitTestSession` resets the dispatcher after a test; two cannot load the Windows ICU ABI name `icu.dll`. The affected case names, full stacks and outcomes are retained in `GAP-2-evidence/app.trx`. These cases are **verification-blocked**, not asserted passing. Existing CRLF files retain their line endings; the whitespace check passes with `git -c core.whitespace=cr-at-eol diff --check`. No font substitution, fixture expectation relaxation, skip addition or production workaround was committed.

A supplemental isolated run of `GapCustomizationAcceptanceTests` used an executor-only `libicu.dll.so` bridge exporting the eight actual ICU calls to Debian's versioned **ICU 76** symbols. It uses the real ICU implementation, with no fake values; the assembly source and build command are retained in evidence. That run has **1 pass (CZ-T36 no preview I/O/draft mutation), 2 assertion failures (CZ-T31 and placeholder-out CZ-T40), and 1 glyphTypeface block (detail-out)**. It duplicates primary cases and is not added to the primary totals. It establishes managed preview isolation on this executor, not Windows/native parity. The new 472-px plate-render comparison passes in the primary run; the separate existing text/control-pixel comparison is ICU-blocked there.

The executor exposes identical proxy values under both upper- and lowercase environment keys. Earlier over-broad selection reached unrelated provider tests that reject duplicate case-insensitive keys. Final runners retain the configured uppercase proxy values and remove only identical lowercase aliases; no proxy/CA route is bypassed. Evidence includes the exact final argv arrays and runner, without credential values. TRX and log lines have trailing whitespace trimmed for Git; outcomes and stack contents are unchanged.

`GAP-2-evidence/acceptance-ledger.json` accounts for all 122 IDs; `trait-additions.json` lists every added existing-test locator. `manifest.json` records source/evidence hashes, test counts and limitations. Independent read-only review confirmed the four defects, the preview's registered real runtime plus all-URI throwing handler, no assertion weakening, and no production changes. GAP-1's unrelated allocation/cloud-key/strike findings remain historical and were not counted as defects newly found here. No STATE, parity status, gate marker or production file was changed. `mark.mjs DONE` is not used for this intentionally failing coverage task. Only `codex/gap-2` is pushed; the delivery chat reports the final head SHA.
